# Consolidation changes

Counts are places, not changed tokens. Reading/state corrections exclude certainty-only decisions and purely syntactic or layout repairs. One place may differ from both witnesses. The six H description-only pages produce no H-collation rows. On transcribed pages, an explicit quoted source reading within a note is comparable; editorial prose itself is excluded. C/H syntax-only differences are not corrections. Source rows were parsed as literal tab-separated lines because raw quotation marks are text.

## Consequential mathematical readings

- Scan 31, Part b display, first raised mark after f: f^{\handwritten{'}} — The ink has a lone prime-like mark where H supplies a pullback star. The domain check would call for f-star on this side, but the transcription retains the prime; see check event 31-M02.
- Scan 31, Part b display, cancelled material at right end: \handwritten{\unsure{\deleted{=g_{s'}}|\deleted{=}g_{s'}|\deleted{=g_*}|\deleted{g_*}}} — The overwritten right tail admits g with an s-prime index and uncertain cancellation extent, as well as g-star alternatives. This preserves source layers omitted or simplified by the witnesses; it does not restore an expected base-change formula.
- Scan 39, Bottom diagram, last object: X_o — The last object of the three-arrow/two-arrow diagram has a keyed lowercase o, whereas C and H supply zero. Handwritten prose zeroes on the same page remain distinct.
- Scan 40, Opening formula: X_2=(X_1,p_2)\mathbin{\mathrm{x}}_{X_o}(X_1,p_1) — The opening product formula retains a keyed lowercase-o base and a roman x-shaped product mark; H regularizes both. The repeated X_o forms in 10.4(iii), including the final morphism target, follow the same visible notation.
- Scan 40, 10.4(iii), tuple (X_1,p_1,p_2): (X_1,p_1,p_{2_{\deleted{\unsure{2|0}}}}) — A cancelled lower-level digit survives beneath the subscript 2 of p_2. Its 2/0 identity remains uncertain; H omits that source layer.
- Scan 43, Circled Kummer notation, mu: \handwritten{\mathbb{\mu}}_n — Two left uprights distinguish the manuscript’s doubled-stroke mu from H’s ordinary mu. The n index remains lowered.
- Scan 43, cd index, cancelled-l versus loopless-ell state: \operatorname{cd}_{\unsure{\deleted{l}|\ell}}(X) — Remaining state uncertainty, excluded from definite-error totals: the lowered l can be cancelled or converted to a loopless ell. The same doubt governs X_c on this page; no extra ell is supplied.
- Scan 45, circled delta formula: possible earlier letter: \unsure{\deleted{d}\handwritten{\delta}|\deleted{Q}\handwritten{\delta}|\handwritten{\delta}}(F,f,y) — Remaining underlayer uncertainty, excluded from definite-error totals: the thin loop beside delta can be cancelled d, cancelled Q, or retracing. No separate old character is forced.
- Scan 66, p.5 li.8: raised mark over alpha: \handwritten{\alpha}^{\unsure{\deleted{x}|\deleted{i}|*|x}}(x)\handwritten{\in}\operatorname{Filt}^{i}(Z) — Remaining exponent/state uncertainty, excluded from definite-error totals: cancelled x or i and live asterisk or x remain possible. The expected mathematics does not select an exponent.
- Scan 68, p.29, raised H exponent, complete formula: H^{\unsure{0|o}}(X,\underline{Z}) — Remaining exponent uncertainty, excluded from definite-error totals: the raised closed round glyph is zero/o. No cohomological degree is supplied from expectation.

Reason: The red rising slash crosses both text lines, but the leaf alone does not determine its function. C uncertainty is retained.. Crop: crops/B37-02_37_scan034_r1_01_first_paragraph.jpg.
- Place: first paragraph, wrote. Reading: wr\unsure{\deleted{i}o|\deleted{o}i}te. Alternatives: wr\deleted{i}ote; wr\deleted{o}ite. Confidence: medium. Reason: Both glyph components are visible; chronology is not.. Crop: crops/B37-02_37_scan034_r1_09_wrote_overtyping.jpg.

- Place: second paragraph, source line 6, treatment. Reading: treat\unsure{\deleted{em}me|\deleted{me}em}nt. Alternatives: treat\deleted{em}ment; treat\deleted{me}emnt. Confidence: medium. Reason: Two consecutive positions each contain e and m components. The two whole correction orders are coupled so that no impossible same-letter combination is presented. Their order of typing remains visually undecidable.. Crop: crops/B37-02_37_scan034_r1_07_overtyping.jpg.
- Place: source line 5, numeral 7. Reading: \handwritten{7}. Alternatives: handwritten 7; typewritten 7. Confidence: medium. Reason: The thick angular black numeral can be a keyed impression or a filled blank. The numeral itself is unequivocally 7; C handwriting wrapper is retained. This is a medium attribution only, not a lexical doubt.. Crop: crops/B37-02_37_scan034_r1_04_reference_p7.jpg.

### Places still unresolved

- Place: Ax title, overtyped letter in dimension. Reading: dime\unsure{\deleted{s}n|\deleted{a}n|\deleted{n}n|\deleted{n}a}sion. Alternatives: s beneath n; a beneath n; n beneath n; n beneath a. Confidence: low. Reason: The crowded cell retains the previously admitted old-letter candidates. The lower rounded form also permits a/n in the reverse order; the crop does not establish chronology. Complete alternatives retain both layers instead of inferring the later letter from the familiar title.. Crop: crops/B37-02_37_scan035_r2_B13.jpg.
- Place: French paragraph, deleted symbol before ell-groups. Reading: \deleted{$\unsure{p|m}$}. Alternatives: p; m. Confidence: low. - Place: Douady reference, initial d in des. Reading: \unsure{\deleted{e}d|\deleted{a}d|\deleted{d}e}es. Alternatives: e beneath d; a beneath d; d beneath e. Confidence: medium. - Place: Douady reference, first accent in théorème. Reading: th\handwritten{\unsure{ê|é}}or\handwritten{è}me. Alternatives: ê; é. Confidence: medium. Reason: A short steep left arm joins a flatter right arm. The first-pass confident acute is withdrawn; the roof-like and curved-acute readings remain coupled.. - Place: Diagram, vertical sign between the two barred G objects. Reading: \unsure{\Vert|k}. Alternatives: vertical equality; k-shaped sign. Confidence: medium. Reason: The left upright is clear; the right stroke has an angled return that can look like k. Equal barred-G objects favour the vertical equality interpretation, but the ink does not justify suppressing the alternative. The sign remains between the rows, without a supplied arrowhead.. Crop: crops/B37-02_37_scan035_r2_B05.jpg.
- Place: Tsen reference, overlapping f/k cell. Reading: Funktionen\unsure{\deleted{f}k|\deleted{k}f}örper. Alternatives: f beneath k; k beneath f. Confidence: medium. Reason: Both letter components are visible. Their order is not established by the overlap, and the familiar German word cannot decide it.. Crop: crops/B37-02_37_scan035_r2_B20.jpg.

### Places still unresolved

- Place: Definition, cancelled mark after algébrique. Reading: \deleted{\unsure{.}}. Alternatives: A full stop is plausible; exact underlying punctuation not separable. Confidence: medium. Reason: Cancellation overlays the low mark; C doubt retained.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan040_r1_03_definition.jpg.
- Place: Initial of Une. Reading: \unsure{\deleted{B}U|\deleted{U}B|\deleted{S}U|\deleted{U}S}ne. Alternatives: \deleted{B}Une; \deleted{U}Bne; \deleted{S}Une; \deleted{U}Sne. Confidence: low. Reason: The overlapping capital preserves an open U outline and upper/lower bowls. Those bowls can belong to S while U supplies the left upright; B remains possible. The ink does not establish impression order. Keep B/U and S/U in both complete deleted/live orders, without selecting Une from grammar. The second-reading expansion adds no new correction place; the source witnesses still omit the explicit overlapping layer already counted here.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan040_r2_B06.jpg.
- Place: Initial of first Homtoplocan. Reading: \uline{\deleted{\unsure{g|p}}Homtoplocan}. Alternatives: Underlying g; Underlying p; Possibly an unidentified overlapping impression. Confidence: low. Reason: Rounded inner impression and descending curved stroke remain beyond the ordinary H; identity not securely separable.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan040_r1_08_homtoplocan.jpg.
- Place: Reference after point géométrique. Reading: \handwritten{\unsure{11b|61b|\deleted{8}10}}. Alternatives: 11b; 61b; \deleted{8}10. Confidence: low. Reason: The B17 detail shows two loops in the first left cluster crossed or overlapped by a longer rising stroke. The final loop has an upward lead compatible with b or 0. Keep 11b and 61b together with the complete cancelled-8 followed by 10 reading; neither the glyph sequence nor the layer order is securely recoverable. No published reference was used. C already includes 11b, so the expanded alternative set is a certainty change against C, not an additional C reading error.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan040_r2_B17_detail.jpg.
- Place: Overtype in monomorphisme. Reading: monomorp\unsure{\deleted{y}h|\deleted{h}y|\deleted{g}h|\deleted{m}h|\deleted{h}h}\unsure{\deleted{ç}i|\deleted{i}ç|\deleted{j}i|\deleted{i}i}sme. Alternatives: First cell: y beneath h, h beneath y, g beneath h, m beneath h, h beneath h; Second cell: ç beneath i, i beneath ç, j beneath i, i beneath i. Confidence: low. Reason: The first overloaded cell has a descender and upper fork compatible with y overlapping h; g, m and repeated h are not fully excluded. The next cell has a curved c-shaped body/cedilla and a dotted i, compatible with ç/i; j and repeated i remain possible. Layer order is unresolved in both cells. The B22 detail supports y-to-h and ç-to-i as preferred ink candidates, not grammatical repair. Record complete paired alternatives for each cell, retaining the earlier candidates.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan040_r2_B22_detail.jpg.
- Place: First équivalence overtype, impression order. Reading: d'équ\unsure{\deleted{k}i|\deleted{i}k}valence. Alternatives: d'équ\deleted{k}ivalence; d'équ\deleted{i}kvalence. Confidence: medium. Reason: The diagonal arms of k and the detached dot/stem of i overlap in the same cell. Their identity is visible but the order of impressions is not recoverable. Keep complete k-beneath-i and i-beneath-k alternatives at this occurrence. C already preserves the k layer; opening its order is certainty-only against C. - Place: Second équivalence overtype, impression order. Reading: d'équ\unsure{\deleted{k}i|\deleted{i}k}valence. Alternatives: d'équ\deleted{k}ivalence; d'équ\deleted{i}kvalence. Confidence: medium. ### Places still unresolved

- Place: p.32, handwritten relation between Der(Gl(n)) and Sl(n). Reading: \underline{\operatorname{Der}}(\operatorname{Gl}(\underline{n}))\handwritten{\unsure{\lhook\joinrel\relbar|\subset|\longleftarrow}}\operatorname{Sl}(\underline{n}). Alternatives: \lhook\joinrel\relbar; \subset; \longleftarrow. Confidence: medium. Reason: The visible sign has a curved left end, a very short upper return and a long lower horizontal stroke. No distinct arrowhead is established. The injection wording does not decide the direction or add an arrowhead. The complete formula was read on the crop, including all underlines and parentheses.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan068_r1_09_der_relation.jpg.
- Place: p.35, li.12 du bas, overtyped page digit. Reading: p.\handwritten{3\unsure{\deleted{4}5|\deleted{5}4}},. Alternatives: 3\deleted{4}5; 3\deleted{5}4. Confidence: low. Reason: The crowded final numeral cell contains a top bar/lower bowl consistent with 5 and an upright/cross-stroke consistent with 4. Their order is not established by the superposition. Keep complete cancelled-4/live-5 and cancelled-5/live-4 alternatives after 3, without a guessed chronology.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan068_r2_B11.jpg.
- Place: Final paragraph, pale trace directly above and behind the comma after U. Reading: $U$,. Alternatives: No additional current-face text; comma only; Pale trace may be an earlier keyed k, but current-face and cancellation status are unestablished. Confidence: low. Reason: The dark U and comma are certain. The much paler k-like upright/diagonals behind the comma cannot be assigned securely to this face or to a cancelled source layer. No cancellation crosses that possible character unambiguously. Retain the prior explicit review observation rather than add a definite or doubtful cancelled k to the source text.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan068_r2_B13.jpg.
- Place: p.26, first word of cancelled instruction. Reading: \deleted{\unsure{vider}}. Alternatives: vider, interiors partly masked. Confidence: medium. Reason: The keyed cancellation crosses cover the interiors of v and e in the first word. Vider remains the preferred reading, but not every letter is independently plain. Add an uncertainty wrapper to that word only; the quoted quelconque and the full cancellation extent remain unchanged.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan068_r2_B02.jpg.
- Place: p.29, raised H exponent in the full formula. Reading: H^{\unsure{0|o}}(X,\underline{Z}). Alternatives: 0; o. Confidence: medium. Reason: The complete H/Gamma formula has a small raised closed round glyph whose shape permits both zero and lowercase o. Keep zero first, without choosing from the expected cohomology degree. The two curved insertion branches and the small separate terminal stroke are all visible: retain the existing three paths and their gap, rather than reduce them to the second reader’s two paths.. Crop: crops/B37-03_37_scan068_r2_B04.jpg.
